```text
/**

import { digestFor, readCassette } from './cassette.ts';
import { LlmError } from './types.ts';
import type { CassetteEntry } from './cassette.ts';
import type { LlmClient, LlmRequest, LlmStreamEvent } from './types.ts';

/** 回放分片的默认粒度，约等于一个短句。 */
export const REPLAY_CHUNK_SIZE = 24;

const BREAK_CHARS = ['\n', '。', '！', '？', '；', '，', '.', '!', '?', ';', ','];

/**
 * 把整段文本切成确定性分片。
 * 回放不该假装"耗时"，但前端仍然需要流式观感，
 * 所以分片规则必须是纯函数：同样的文本永远切出同样的分片。
 */
export function chunkText(text: string, size: number = REPLAY_CHUNK_SIZE): string[] {
  const chars = Array.from(text);
  const out: string[] = [];
  let buf: string[] = [];
  const softMin = Math.floor(size / 2);

  for (const ch of chars) {
    buf.push(ch);
    const atBreak = BREAK_CHARS.indexOf(ch) >= 0 && buf.length >= softMin;
    if (buf.length >= size || atBreak) {
      out.push(buf.join(''));
      buf = [];
    }
  }
  if (buf.length > 0) out.push(buf.join(''));
  return out;
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me plan the offline demo second run. Goal: the demo should show "改完再跑一遍" comparison. The cleanest approach: in demo-seed, after running the first session with accepted revisions applied, run a second session using revised resume text and parentSessionId = first session. The scripted client must produce a plan for the second run where C2 becomes solid.

But careful: the script for the second session must handle a resume where the text has changed. 1. Add `DEMO_REVISED` info. Rather than a parallel structure, extend DemoClaimPlan with optional `revised?: DemoRevisedPlan` where:
```ts
export interface DemoRevisedPlan {
  /** 采纳改写后，这句话在简历里可能长什么样 —— 三个风格档位各一句（顺序无关） */
  quotes: readonly string[];
  verdict: DemoVerdictPlan;
  answer: DemoAnswerPlan;
  questions: readonly string[];
  probeRationale: string;
}
```
Wait, quotes must be derivable from suggestion.afterByStyle + after. Duplication risk! The suggestion's `after` values already exist. If revised.quotes duplicates them, a drift could occur. Better: `revised` doesn't list quotes; instead the matcher derives candidate quotes from `plan.suggestion` (after + afterByStyle values). That's DRY and guarantees "if the resume contains the suggested text, we recognize it".

So:
```ts
export interface DemoClaimPlan extends DemoClaimDraft {
  questions, probeRationale, answer, verdict, suggestion,
  /** 采纳改写之后再跑一遍，这条主张长成了什么样子 */
  revised?: {
    /** 认出来之后，主张文本用这句（比原文更收敛）；引文仍用简历里的那句 */
    text: string;
    questions: readonly string[];
    probeRationale: string;
    answer: DemoAnswerPlan;
    verdict: DemoVerdictPlan;
  } | null;
}
```
And matching: `revisedQuotesOf(plan)` = [suggestion.after, ...Object.values(suggestion.afterByStyle)] filtered non-empty.

For run 2, checking substring presence in the prompt is a good approximation, but a quote could be split across rendered lines if the segmenter splits the resume differently...
```ts
interface ScriptState {
  asked: Set<string>;
  /** 这一场用的计划：采纳改写之后再跑一遍时，被改写的那几条换成 revised 版本 */
  plans: DemoClaimPlan[];
}
```

`createDemoScriptedClient(dir, resumeText = DEMO_RESUME)`:
```ts
const state: ScriptState = { asked: new Set<string>(), plans: plansForResume(resumeText) };
```

`plansForResume(resume)`:
```ts
function plansForResume(resumeText: string): DemoClaimPlan[] {
  return DEMO_PLANS.map((plan) => revisedPlanFor(plan, resumeText) ?? plan);
}

function revisedPlanFor(plan, resumeText): DemoClaimPlan | null {
  const revised = plan.revised;
  if (revised === null || revised === undefined || plan.suggestion === null) return null;
  // 原文还在，就不算改过
  if (resolveAnchor(resumeText, plan.quote).ok) return null;
  for (const quote of revisedQuotesOf(plan)) {
    if (!resolveAnchor(resumeText, quote).ok) continue;
    return {
      ...plan,
      text: revised.text,
      quote,
      questions: [...revised.questions],
      probeRationale: revised.probeRationale,
      answer: revised.answer,
      verdict: { kind: 'solid', confidence: revised.confidence, rationale: revised.rationale, evidenceQuotes: [quote] },
      suggestion: null,
    };
  }
  return null;
}
```

```text
/**
 * 证据锚定（创新点 1）。
 *
 * 这是整个项目的**信任根**：所有提问、判定、修改建议都必须携带一个能指回简历原文的
 * 字符区间。锚点由本模块建立，而本模块**只认逐字引用**——AI 报出的 start/end 一律不被信任，
 * 我们拿它的 quote 回到原文里重新定位。找不到就拒绝，并把拒绝原因留给前端展示。
 *
 * 这样做的直接效果：AI 无法提问一条简历里根本不存在的内容。
 */
import type { Anchor, TextSpan } from './domain.ts';

export interface RawAnchor {
  quote?: string;
  start?: number;
  end?: number;
}

export type AnchorRejection =
  | 'empty-quote'
  | 'quote-not-found'
  | 'too-short'
  | 'out-of-range';

export type AnchorResult =
  | { ok: true; anchor: Anchor }
  | { ok: false; reason: AnchorRejection; quote: string };

export const REJECTION_LABEL: Record<AnchorRejection, string> = {
  'empty-quote': '模型没有给出引用原文',
  'quote-not-found': '引用内容在简历原文中找不到（疑似编造）',
  'too-short': '引用过短，无法作为证据',
  'out-of-range': '给出的偏移量超出简历范围',
};

/** 归一化时做的标点统一，只为兜住全半角/中英标点差异，不改变语义 */
const PUNCT_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  '\uFF0C': ',', '\u3002': '.', '\uFF1A': ':', '\uFF1B': ';',
  '\uFF08': '(', '\uFF09': ')', '\u201C': '"', '\u201D': '"',
  '\u2018': "'", '\u2019': "'", '\uFF01': '!', '\uFF1F': '?',
  '\u3001': ',', '\u2014': '-', '\u2013': '-', '\u3000': ' ', '\u00B7': '.',
};

/**
 * 归一化文本，并保留「归一化下标 -> 原文下标」的映射，
 * 这样命中后还能把区间还原回原文坐标（前端要高亮
…（此处省略 2798 字符，原始长度 3998）
```

```text
362|  return null;
363|}
364|
365|/** 用量是**估算值**（按字符数折半），不是真实计费数据；演示卡带不假装自己是账单 */
366|function estimateUsage(req: LlmRequest, text: string, reasoning: string): LlmUsage {
367|  let promptChars = req.system.length;
368|  for (const message of req.messages) promptChars += message.content.length;
369|  return {
370|    inputTokens: Math.max(1, Math.round(promptChars / 2)),
371|    outputTokens: Math.max(1, Math.round(text.length / 2)),
372|    cacheReadTokens: 0,
373|    reasoningTokens: Math.round(reasoning.length / 2),
374|  };
375|}
376|
377|function extractText(): string {
378|  const claims: DemoClaimDraft[] = [];
379|  for (const plan of DEMO_PLANS) {
380|    claims.push({ text: plan.text, quote: plan.quote, category: plan.category, weight: plan.weight });
381|  }
382|  claims.push(DEMO_UNANCHORED_DRAFT);
383|  return JSON.stringify({ claims });
384|}
385|
386|/** 提示词里每条主张的渲染形状：'[C1] 类别=metric 权重=0.95' + '主张：…' + '原文引文：…' */
387|const CLAIM_BLOCK = /\[(C[0-9]+)\][^\n]*\n\s*主张：[^\n]*\n\s*原文引文：([^\n]*)
…（此处省略 8481 字符，原始长度 9681）
```

</details>

Let me design a helper:

```ts
interface RevisedInfo { plan: DemoClaimPlan; quote: string; style: RewriteStyle }
function revisedIn(text: string): RevisedInfo | null {
  for (const plan of DEMO_PLANS) {
    const s = plan.suggestion;
    if (s === null) continue;
    const variants: [RewriteStyle, string][] = [
      ['conservative', s.afterByStyle.conservative ?? s.after],
      ['bold', s.afterByStyle.bold ?? s.after],
      ['normal', s.after],
    ];
    for (const [style, quote] of variants) if (text.indexOf(quote) >= 0) return { plan, quote, style };
  }
  return null;
}
```

Now let me think about the design of the offline demo second round.

The key question: should I do the "second round" in the offline demo at all? It's a lot of complexity: cassettes would need to cover both original and revised resume texts, meaning extract/probe/answer/verdict/suggest with different resume content → different digests → 9 combos × 2 rounds = cassette explosion. Actually the second round's resume text differs per style (since suggestions differ per style), so each style has its own second-round. So 9 combos first round + 9 combos second round (with per-style revised text). But note probing/answer/verdict prompts include resume text, so all differ.

That's roughly doubling: 56 → ~112+ cassettes. Time: seed currently takes how long? Let me measure. Actually the important thing for C1 is that the offline demo (the thing judges see) demonstrates the version comparison. Without it, a judge in offline replay mode would have to manually accept revisions and click "改完再跑一遍" — which would immediately hit "cassette-missing" errors, since the revised resume text isn't in any cassette. That's a BAD experience: judges click the button and get an error.

That's a serious product problem. Options:
1. Make the offline demo cover the second round (full coverage), doubling cassettes.
2. On re-run with no cassette, fall back gracefully: show a friendly notice "离线演示没有录这一版" — but that undermines the feature.
3. Have the demo seed run only the default combo twice (not all 9), and in other combos... still broken.

Hmm. Actually, wait. Let's think about what gets into cassettes. Second-round resume text = applied revisions of round 1 with style X. Since each of the 9 combos has its own revised text, and each of the 9 combos needs full second round coverage (extract/jd-extract/jd-merge/jd-match/probe/answer/verdict/suggest/counter), that's ~9 more "full runs" of cassettes. Cassettes per run: extract 1, jd-extract 1, jd-merge 1, jd-match 1, probe ~24 (per round per interviewer), answer 10, verdict 5, suggest 12, counter 1. After dedupe across 9 combos it was 56. Doubling gives ~112.

Time cost: seed runs each combo twice (record + replay) already. Adding second round doubles → seed time ×2. Let me measure current seed time first.

Actually, hold on. Maybe a smarter approach: make the second round's scripted response depend only on *content* that's stable. But the digest includes the resume text, so inevitably new cassettes.

Alternative smarter approach: instead of literally re-running the whole pipeline, the demo could include a **pre-seeded comparison**: i.e., the seed creates the first session AND a second session with parentSessionId, and the demo UI shows the comparison from a demo session. Hmm, but the judge's flow is: click "载入示例" → run → accept revisions → click "改完再跑一遍". To make that work offline, the cassettes for the second run must exist.

Wait — actually there's a subtlety. Does accept-all in the demo produce exactly the revised text that seed used? Yes if seed does the same: acceptRevisions(db, id, all claim ids with suggestions) for that style → revised text. Then second run uses that text. As long as seed uses the same accept-all path (not per-claim subsets), the digest matches for the "accept all" flow. Partial acceptance would produce different text → cassette missing. That's acceptable and should be documented; but the UI should show a friendly message.

Hmm, but is the doubled cassette cost acceptable? 336K → ~700K, still trivial. Seed time doubles. Let's measure seed time.

Let me also consider: is there an alternative that demonstrates comparison offline without doubling? E.g., the demo seed could produce a second session but the *comparison* is computed by pure code (compareRuns) from the two sessions' claim/verdict rows — that part needs no LLM. So what needs cassettes is only the *second run's* LLM calls.

Actually, another thought: what if the second run's resume text is short-circuited? No — the pipeline must run.

OK so option 1 it is, if seed time is acceptable. Let me measure seed time now.

Actually wait. There's another approach that costs much less: make the **second run reuse the same resume text**? No, then nothing changes.

Alternatively: the second run in the demo could use a *deliberately simplified* path... no, hacky.

In replay mode, that second session's LLM calls must find cassettes. Those cassettes must exist. So seed must produce them.

The revised text depends on which suggestions were accepted. In demo, the UI "全部采纳" accepts all 4 suggestions (C2..C5). Then revised.text = application of all 4 replacements to DEMO_RESUME. That's deterministic given the style! Note: the `after` text varies by style (3 styles). So revised text differs across styles. And applying revisions must be valid (before anchors to original resume).

So seed would run: for each (difficulty, style) combination: run session1 (existing), then build revised text by accepting all claims with suggestions, then run session2 with resumeText=revised, parentSessionId=session1.

```text
8| * 一个刻意的设计：面试官是**并发**发问的，但记账是**串行**的。
9| * 并发的意义是演示时四路流式同时吐字；串行的意义是预算账本不依赖时序，
10| * 于是同一盘卡带无论跑多少次，账本和主张判定都完全一致。
11| *
12| * 错误处理的分级也很刻意：卡带缺失、配置不全这类属于"环境坏了"，直接抛出，
13| * 绝不退化成"这场没有追问"；单个面试官的网络抖动则只记在该面试官的 outcome 里，
14| * 其他面试官照常发问。
15| */
16|
```

Pseudo:
```ts
async function recordPair(dir, scriptedV1, scriptedV2, difficulty, style) {
  const db = openDatabase({path:':memory:'});
  try {
    const firstId = newSessionId(db, 'record', difficulty, style, DEMO_RESUME);
    const first = await runAndPersist({db, sessionId: firstId, client: scriptedV1.client, model: 'scripted-demo'});
    const accepted = acceptRevisions(db, firstId, DEMO_PLANS.map(p => claimId...)); // need claim ids: C1..C5
    const revised = revisedOf(db, firstId);
    const secondId = newSessionId(db, 'record', difficulty, style, revised.text, firstId);
    const second = await runAndPersist({db, sessionId: secondId, client: scriptedV2.client, model: 'scripted-demo'});
    const comparison = compareSessions(db, secondId);
    return {first, second, revised, comparison};
  } finally { closeDatabase(db); }
}
```
